- **sweep-auditor**: Completeness adversary - finds the member of a should-be-uniform set that diverged (the incomplete sweep: a change applied to N-1 of N sites). Dispatch after a migration / rename / new-variant / dual-surface change, during audits, or from the idle loop. Writes a completeness guard; the deliverable is a straggler (a bug) or a durable exhaustiveness test. (#1826 family - the fourth orthogonal shape.)
